\displaystyle\times\frac{\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}+\delta}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}+\delta}}{D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}+D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi.

By virtue of Proposition
3.7
we can bound the denominator as

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta_{1},\phi)-\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta_{2},\phi)\right|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}

\displaystyle+C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)||h_{2}(\Phi)|

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\frac{\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}}{(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}}{(\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{\delta}}{(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{2}^{\delta}}{(\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\Bigg)dbd\Phi.

Let us show the bound for the third integral, and the others will follow similarly. Using now that
h_{1}\in\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}
and then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}+\delta}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)||h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq C||h_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\beta_{1}^{\delta}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}/(1+b)^{2\sigma}|h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{(\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq C||h_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\beta_{1}^{\delta}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta_{2}^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}/(1+b)^{2\sigma}|h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{(\beta_{2}^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{1-\nu}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{1-\nu}}|\sin((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)|^{\nu}\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{\gamma}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{\gamma}}}

for
\nu\in(0,1)
with

(66)

{1-\nu+\gamma\in(0,1).}

Hence

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}+\delta}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)||h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq C||h_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\beta_{1}^{\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta_{2}^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}/(1+b)^{2\sigma}}{(\beta_{2}^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{1-\nu}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{1-\nu}}\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{\gamma}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{\gamma}}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}|\sin^{-\nu}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)||h_{2}(\Phi)|d\Phi db

Note that by interpolation we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}|\sin^{-\nu}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)||h_{2}(\Phi)|d\Phi\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\int_{0}^{2\pi}|h_{2}(\Phi)|^{p}d\Phi\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\int_{0}^{2\pi}|\sin^{-\nu}((\Phi)/2)|^{q}d\Phi\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

(67)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}},

provided that
\nu q<1
and that
\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}=1
. By using (
66
), we can choose any
p
such that

(68)

\frac{1}{1-\gamma}<p.

Finally, using the change of variables
b=\beta_{2}z
we get:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}+\delta}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)||h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

(69)

\displaystyle\leq C||h_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}}\beta_{1}^{\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta_{2}^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}/(1+b)^{2\sigma}}{(\beta_{2}^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{1-\nu}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{1-\nu}}\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{\gamma}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{\gamma}}}db

\displaystyle\leq C||h_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}}\beta_{2}^{\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{z^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(z\beta_{2})^{\sigma}/(1+z\beta_{2})^{2\sigma}}{(1+z^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\frac{|\beta_{1}-z\beta_{2}|^{1-\nu}}{|\beta_{1}+z\beta_{2}|^{1-\nu}}\frac{|1-z|^{\gamma}}{|1+z|^{\gamma}}}dz

\displaystyle\leq C||h_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}},

where we have used that
\beta_{1}<\beta_{2}
, and which is bounded provided that
\sigma
verifies (
58
) and (
59
) and

\delta<\sigma,

in order to get the boundedness as
\beta_{2}
goes to
\infty
. Hence, putting everything together we find that

|\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq C||h_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}}\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}},

implying that
H_{f}\in C^{\delta}_{\beta}
.

\bullet

\mathcal{H}_{f}\in\textnormal{Lip}_{\phi}
.

We shall prove that

(70)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)-\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)|\leq C|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|.

For that we need to estimate the difference of the denominator at two points:

\displaystyle D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)=\left(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi_{1})\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi_{2})\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi_{1})\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\cos(\phi_{1}+\beta-\Phi-b))

\displaystyle-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi_{2})\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\cos(\phi_{2}+\beta-\Phi-b)).

Hence we can achieve that

\displaystyle|D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)|\leq C|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|{(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})}

(71)

\displaystyle\times(D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}+D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Hence

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}-\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\right|\leq\frac{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}-D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{|D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)|}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}+D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}},

and using (
3.3
) we find

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}-\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|\frac{{(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})}}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}+D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}.

In that way, we obtain that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)-\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}{(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)|h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}+D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}{(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)|h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{D(f)(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}D(f)(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}{(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)|h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{\left\{\beta^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\right\}\left\{\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\right\}\frac{|\beta-b|^{\gamma}}{|\beta+b|^{\gamma}}\left\{\frac{|\beta-b|^{2}}{|\beta+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta-b+\phi_{1}-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}{b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)||h_{2}(\Phi)|dbd\Phi}{\left\{\beta^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\right\}\frac{|\beta-b|^{\gamma+1-\nu}}{|\beta+b|^{\gamma+1-\nu}}|\sin((\beta-b+\phi_{1}-\Phi)/2)|^{\nu}}.

Using now (
3.3
) we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)-\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C||h_{1}||_{X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}}|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}/(1+b)^{2\sigma}db}{\left\{\beta^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\right\}\frac{|\beta-b|^{\gamma+1-\nu}}{|\beta+b|^{\gamma+1-\nu}}},

for
p
with
\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}=1
and
q\nu<1
as done in (
3.3
).
Doing now the change of variable
b=z\beta
amounts to

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta,\phi_{1},b,\Phi)-\mathcal{H}_{f}(\beta,\phi_{2},b,\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C||h_{1}||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}}|\sin((\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})/2)|\beta^{\sigma}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{z^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}z^{\sigma}/(1+z\beta)^{2\sigma}dz}{\left\{1+z^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\right\}\frac{|1-z|^{\gamma+1-\nu}}{|1+z|^{\gamma+1-\nu}}}

\displaystyle\ \leq

\displaystyle C||h_{1}||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}},

provided that
\gamma+1-\nu<1
,
p
satisfies (
68
) and
\sigma
satisfies (
58
) and (
59
).

\bullet

\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{H}_{f}\in L^{\infty}_{\sigma}
.

By using the expression of
\mathcal{H}_{f}
we find

\displaystyle\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)=

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\frac{1}{\beta}\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma}{2}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}h_{1}(b,\Phi)h_{2}(\Phi)\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi.

Note that

\displaystyle\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)=

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma}{2}\beta\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}h_{1}(b,\Phi)h_{2}(\Phi)\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

(72)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1}+\mathcal{H}_{2},

where the first term was already studied in the previous steps. For the second term, let us compute the derivative of the denominator as follows:

\displaystyle\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)=2\left(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\frac{\partial_{\varphi}\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)}{2\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{-1}{2}}\partial_{\varphi}\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b))

(73)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{1+\alpha}\frac{1}{\beta}\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)),

implying that

(74)

\displaystyle|\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq CD(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\frac{1}{\beta}.

In this way, we find that

\displaystyle\left|\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{H}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)\right|\leq C|\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)|

\displaystyle+C\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|h_{1}(b,\phi+\beta-\eta-b)||h_{2}(\phi+\beta-\eta-b)|}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\phi+\beta-\eta-b)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}dbd\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)|

\displaystyle+C||h_{1}||_{X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}}\beta^{\sigma}\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{z^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}z^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}/(1+\beta z)^{2\sigma}|h_{2}(\phi+\beta-\eta-b)|}{(\beta^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta-b|^{2}}{|\beta+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}(\eta/2)\right\}^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}dbd\eta.

Using (
3.3
) we get

\displaystyle\left|\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{H}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)\right|\leq

\displaystyle C|\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})(\beta,\phi)|

\displaystyle+C||h_{1}||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}}\beta^{\sigma}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{z^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}z^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}/(1+\beta z)^{2\sigma}}{(\beta^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}})\frac{|\beta-b|^{1+\gamma-\nu}}{|\beta+b|^{1+\gamma-\nu}}}db.

Then:

(75)

\displaystyle||\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{H}(h_{1},h_{2})||_{\sigma}\leq C||h_{1}||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}||h_{2}||_{X_{2}^{p}},

provided that (
58
) and (
59
) hold, as well as (
68
) and
1+\gamma-\nu<1
.

\bullet

\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})\in C^{\delta}_{\beta}
.

Since
\mathcal{H}_{f}(h_{1},h_{2})\in C^{\delta}_{\beta}
, the first term in (
3.3
) does. Hence, it remains to check the second term in (
3.3
):
\mathcal{H}_{2}
. Take
\beta_{1}<\beta_{2}
, and then they satisfy (
61
). Let us write the difference of
\mathcal{H}_{2}
in these two terms as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{2}(\beta_{1})-\mathcal{H}_{2}(\beta_{2})=C\beta_{1}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}h_{1}(b,\Phi)h_{2}(\Phi)\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)}{D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}-\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}\right\}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle+C\beta_{1}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}h_{1}(b,\Phi)h_{2}(\Phi)(\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi))}{D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle+C\beta_{1}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}h_{1}(b,\Phi)h_{2}(\Phi)\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)}{D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}}-\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right\}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle+C(\beta_{1}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}-\beta_{2}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}})\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}h_{1}(b,\Phi)h_{2}(\Phi)\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)}{D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{2,1}+\mathcal{H}_{2,2}+\mathcal{H}_{2,3}+\mathcal{H}_{2,4}.

By using (
65
) and (
74
) we can bound
\mathcal{H}_{2,1}
and
\mathcal{H}_{2,3}
. Let us show the estimate for
\mathcal{H}_{2,1}
. Indeed, from (
3.3
) we find for
\delta=1
that

\displaystyle|D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|}(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}+1}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}+1})

\displaystyle\times(D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}+D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}).

By interpolating with

|D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq|D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)+D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)|,

we find

\displaystyle|D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}+\delta}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}+\delta})

(76)

\displaystyle\times(D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\delta}{2}}+D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\delta}{2}}).

Hence using the ideas in (
65
) together with (
3.3
) we find

(77)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}-\frac{1}{D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}\right|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}

(78)

\displaystyle\times\frac{\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}+\delta}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}+\delta}}{D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\delta}{2}}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}+D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\delta}{2}}D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}.

Hence, (
77
) together with (
74
) amounts to

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}_{2,1}|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{-1}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)||h_{2}(\Phi)|(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}+\delta})dbd\Phi}{D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\delta}{2}}D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|h_{1}(b,\Phi)||h_{2}(\Phi)|(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}+\delta})dbd\Phi}{|\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}}|\left\{\frac{|\beta_{2}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{2}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{2}-b+\phi-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}|\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-1-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}|\left\{\frac{|\beta_{1}-b|^{2}}{|\beta_{1}+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta_{1}-b+\phi-\Phi)/2)\right\}^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}}

Using the ideas for (
3.3
) we finally get

|\mathcal{H}_{2,1}|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}||h_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}||h_{2}||_{L^{p}},

for some constant
C
, provided that
\delta<1-\gamma
,
\delta<\sigma
and
p>\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

Let us move to
\mathcal{H}_{2,3}
and show the main idea. In order to work with that term, we should estimate the difference of
\partial_{\varphi}D(f)
in two points. Note that

\displaystyle\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)=2\left(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\partial_{\varphi}\left[\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle+2\partial_{\varphi}\left[\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)).

By working with that term as for (
3.3
) we obtain

(79)

\displaystyle|\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq C

\displaystyle\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|}

\displaystyle\times(\beta_{1}^{-1}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{-1}\beta_{2}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{1}^{-1}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}+1}+\beta_{2}^{-1}\beta_{2}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}+1}),

which interpolating with (
74
) agrees with

(80)

\displaystyle|\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-\partial_{\varphi}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta}}

\displaystyle\times(\beta_{1}^{-\delta}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-2\delta}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{-\delta}\beta_{2}^{\frac{-2\delta}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{1}^{-\delta}\beta_{1}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}+\delta}+\beta_{2}^{-\delta}\beta_{2}^{\frac{-\delta}{1+\alpha}+\delta})

\displaystyle\times(\beta_{1}^{-\frac{1-\delta}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{1}^{-(1-\delta)}D(f)(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}+\beta_{2}^{-\frac{1-\delta}{1+\alpha}}\beta_{2}^{-(1-\delta)}D(f)(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}).

Then, the key point to bound
\mathcal{H}_{2,2}
is the use of (
80
) instead of just (
79
).

Finally, the estimate for the last term
\mathcal{H}_{2,4}
follows identically as for
\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{H}_{f}\in L^{\infty}_{\sigma}
taking into account that

|\beta_{1}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}-\beta_{2}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|}(\beta_{1}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}})\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|}\beta_{2}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}.

The
C^{1}
regularity of
F
follows similarly by estimating the associated Fréchet derivatives.
∎

In the next proposition, we give the well-definition of the nonlinear function together with its
C^{1}
regularity.

Proposition 3.10
.

Let
\gamma,\sigma\in(0,1)
and
\alpha\in(1,2)
satisfying (
56
). There exists
\epsilon<1
such that
\tilde{F}:B_{X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}}(\epsilon)\times X_{2}^{p}\rightarrow Y^{\sigma,\delta}
is well-defined and
C^{1}
, for any
p>\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
\delta<\min\{1-\gamma,\sigma\}
.

Proof.

Let us prove that
\tilde{F}
is well-defined and
C^{1}
in
f
, where we shall use the expression of
\tilde{F}(f,\Omega)
in (
2.4
). Note that the first term in the expression of
\tilde{F}(f,\Omega)
is just the constant
1
, which is not in
Y^{\sigma,\delta}
since it does not decay appropriately at
0
and
\infty
. For that reason, we need to add and subtract the contribution of the trivial solution in
\tilde{F}
obtaining that

\displaystyle\tilde{F}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)=f(\beta,\phi)-(-1)^{\frac{-1}{2\mu}}\frac{C_{\gamma}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}}{4\pi C_{0}(1+\alpha)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}}(I_{1}(f,\Omega)+I_{2}(f,\Omega)+I_{3}(f,\Omega)+I_{4}(f,\Omega))(\beta,\phi),

where

\displaystyle I_{1}(f,\Omega):=\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\partial_{\overline{\beta\varphi}}f(b,\Phi)\Omega(\Phi)dbd\Phi}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

\displaystyle I_{2}(f,\Omega):=\frac{2(\alpha-1)}{(1+\alpha)^{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\left[\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}-\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\right]\Omega(\Phi)dbd\Phi}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

\displaystyle I_{3}(f,\Omega):=\frac{2(\alpha-1)}{(1+\alpha)^{2}}\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\Omega(\Phi)}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}-D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\}dbd\Phi,

\displaystyle I_{4}(f,\Omega):=\frac{2(\alpha-1)}{(1+\alpha)^{2}}\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)dbd\Phi}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}.

Note that now
f\in Y^{\sigma,\delta}
and it remains to prove that
I_{i}\in Y^{\sigma,\delta}
, for
i=1,2,3,4
.

By using the operator
\mathcal{H}
defined in Proposition
3.9
we can write
I_{1}
,
I_{2}
and
I_{4}
as:

\displaystyle I_{1}(f,\Omega)=

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\mathcal{H}\left(\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}f\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\partial_{\overline{\beta\varphi}}f,\Omega\right),

\displaystyle I_{2}(f,\Omega)=

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}\mathcal{H}\left(\left[\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}f\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}-\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\right],\Omega\right),

for some constants
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}
.
Then, by Proposition
3.9
we find

\displaystyle||I_{1}||_{Y^{\sigma,\delta}}+||I_{2}||_{Y^{\sigma,\delta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C||\Omega||_{X_{2}^{p}}\left\{\left\|\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}f\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\partial_{\overline{\beta\varphi}}f\right\|_{\mathcal{X}_{\sigma,\alpha}}+\left\|\left[\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}f\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}-\left\{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\right]\right\|_{\mathcal{X}_{\sigma,\alpha}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C||\Omega||_{X_{2}^{p}}||f||_{X_{1}^{\sigma,\alpha}}.

Moreover, Proposition
3.9
gives us that
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
are
C^{1}
in
f
. It remains to check the well-posedness and
C^{1}
regularity of
I_{3}
and
I_{4}
. Let us start with
I_{4}
.

Note that

\displaystyle I_{4}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)=

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)dbd\Phi}{\left\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)dbd\Phi}{\left\{\beta^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}I_{4,1}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi),

where
C
is some constant which is not important in this proof. Let us first check the
L^{\infty}_{\sigma}
norm. Take first
\beta\in[0,\beta_{0}]
. Since we have in the numerator
\Omega-1
, we get an extra cancellation at
\beta=0
. This comes from the expression of
\Omega-1
:

\Omega(\phi)-1=\sum_{0\neq k\in\Z}g_{k}e^{ik\phi},

where the mode
k=0
vanishes implying that

\int_{0}^{2\pi}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)d\Phi=0.

In that way, we obtain that

\displaystyle I_{4,1}(f,\Omega)(0,\phi)=

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\varepsilon}^{1/\varepsilon}\frac{b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)dbd\Phi}{\left\{b^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}\int_{\varepsilon}^{1/\varepsilon}\frac{b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}db}{\left\{b^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)d\Phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0.

Then:

\displaystyle\sup_{\beta\in[0,\beta_{0}]}\frac{(1+\beta)^{2\sigma}}{\beta^{\sigma}}I_{4}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)\leq\sup_{\beta\in[0,\beta_{0}]}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}}{\beta^{\sigma}}<C,

since
\sigma<\frac{\gamma+1-\alpha}{1+\alpha}
due to (
56
). Now take
\beta\in[\beta_{0},\infty)
and note that
I_{4}
can be also written as

(81)

\displaystyle I_{4}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)=

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)dbd\Phi}{\left\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}I_{4,2}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi),

where

\lim_{\beta\rightarrow+\infty}I_{4,2}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)=0,

since
\Omega-1
has a vanishing
0
Fourier mode as before. Since
\sigma<\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}
by (
56
) we get

\sup_{\beta\in[\beta_{0},+\infty)}I_{4}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)<C.

Now make the change of variables
b=\beta z
in the expression of
I_{4}
to get

(82)

\displaystyle I_{4}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)=

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{z^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)dbd\Phi}{\left\{1+z^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}-2z^{\frac{1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-z\beta)\right\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

where we can prove easily the
C^{\delta}
regularity in
\beta
and the
Lip
regularity in
\phi
using such expression and the ideas developed in Proposition
3.9
. Taking
\partial_{\varphi}
to (
81
) we find

\displaystyle\partial_{\varphi}I_{4}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)=C\beta^{-1}I_{4}(f,\Omega)(\beta,\phi)

\displaystyle+C\beta^{-1}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)\left\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}dbd\Phi}{\left\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}.

Let us check the
L^{\infty}_{\sigma}
norm of
\beta\partial_{\varphi}I_{4}
. The first term is clearly in
L^{\infty}_{\sigma}
and the second term can be written as

\displaystyle\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\Omega(\Phi)-1)\left\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}dbd\Phi}{\left\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}I_{4,3}(\beta,\phi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}I_{4,4}(\beta,\phi)

with
I_{4,4}(0,\phi)=0
due to the structure of
\Omega-1
, and

\lim_{\beta\rightarrow+\infty}I_{4,3}(\beta,\phi)=0.

Arguing as before for
I_{4}
working with
I_{4,4}
for
\beta\in[0,\beta_{0}]
and with
I_{4,3}
for
\beta\in[\beta_{0},+\infty)
we find that
\beta\partial_{\varphi}I_{4}\in L^{\infty}_{\sigma}
. In the same way we can prove the Hölder continuity of such term and the
C^{1}
regularity in
f
.

Let us work with
I_{3}
. A priori, it can not be written in terms of
\mathcal{H}
, however it has the same scaling and the estimates works similarly. Let us just gave the first ideas to estimate it. Recall

\displaystyle I_{3}(f,\Omega)=

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\Omega(\Phi)}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}-D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\}dbd\Phi.

Notice that

\displaystyle|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}-D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}|\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)|}{|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}+D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}|}.

Via Proposition
3.7
and Remark
3.8
, we have that
D(f)
and
D(0)
are comparable:
D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)\geq D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)
and then

(83)

|I_{3}(f,\Omega)|\leq C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|\Omega(\Phi)|}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\frac{|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)|}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)}dbd\Phi.

Moreover, using the expression of
D(f)
in (
51
) we find

\displaystyle|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq C|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}+D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}|

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\left|\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right|

\displaystyle+\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left|\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right|\left|\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\phi)\right|^{\frac{1}{2}}||\sin((\beta+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)|

\displaystyle+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left|\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left|\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right||\sin((\beta+\phi-b-\Phi)/2)|\Bigg].

Since
\partial_{b}f(\beta,\Phi)
is bounded in
L^{\infty}
we get

\displaystyle|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq C|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}+D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}|

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left|\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right|+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left|\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}+\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right|\Bigg].

Now, we wish to take into account the bound for
f
and hence using that the norm of
f
is small, we can write it as

\displaystyle|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)-D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq

\displaystyle C|D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}+D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}|

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left|\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)\right|+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\left|\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)\right|\Bigg].

In this way, we separate
I_{3}
in two integrals and use again Remark
3.8
to compare
D(f)
with
D(0)
:

\displaystyle|I_{3}(f,\Omega)|\leq

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}|\Omega(\Phi)|}{D(f)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\frac{\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}|\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f(\beta,\phi)|+b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}|\partial_{\overline{b}}f(b,\Phi)|}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}}dbd\Phi.

By using similar arguments than in Proposition
3.9
, we finally achieve that

||I_{3}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\leq C||\Omega||_{L^{p}}||\partial_{\overline{\beta}}f||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}},

for any
p>1
.

By working with
D(f)
and using the ideas of Proposition
3.9
, we can check that the others norms in
X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}
are also bounded.
∎

4.
Linearized operator

This section aims to give different expression of the linear operator of
\tilde{F}
at the trivial solution
(0,1)
. First, we will show that there is an extra cancellation that help us to prove that the linear operator is a compact perturbation of a isomorphism. Hence, we can conclude that the linearized operator is Fredholm of zero index, which will be useful in checking that it is a isomorphism in the following section. Moreover, at the end of this section, we give its expression in Fourier series.

4.1.
Linearization at the equilibrium

First, let us give the expression for the linear operator of
\tilde{F}
at
(0,1)
.

Proposition 4.1
.

The linearized operator of
\tilde{F}
at the trivial solution
(0,1)
reads as follows:

\displaystyle\partial_{f}\tilde{F}(0,1)h(\beta,\phi)=

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\partial_{\beta}(\beta h(\beta,\phi))

\displaystyle-\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\frac{1}{\alpha-1}\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma}{2}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{2\pi C_{0}}\frac{1}{1+\alpha}\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}+1}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi

(84)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\partial_{\beta}(\beta h(\beta,\phi))+I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\partial_{f}\tilde{F}(0,1)h(\beta,\phi)=h(\beta,\phi)-L(h)(\beta,\phi)

\displaystyle-\frac{C_{\gamma}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}}{4\pi C_{0}(1+\alpha)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\mu}\frac{C_{\gamma}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}}{4\pi C_{0}(1+\alpha)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}-1}\left\{\frac{2(\alpha-1)}{(1+\alpha)^{2}}\right\}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma}{2}\frac{C_{\gamma}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}}{4\pi C_{0}(1+\alpha)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}}\left\{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right\}^{-\frac{1}{2\mu}}\left\{\frac{2(\alpha-1)}{(1+\alpha)^{2}}\right\}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}+1}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi

Let us explain the meaning of
L
. The function
L
means the linearization of the local part associated to
-(-\Delta)^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\hat{\theta}
after performing the adapted coordinates and the appropriate scaling from
\Psi
to
f
. That is, take the initial stream function (which is a radial function):

\hat{\psi}^{0}(re^{i\theta})=-C_{0}r^{1-\alpha},

and now we use the fact that
r=(1+\alpha)^{\frac{1}{2}}(-\Psi_{\beta})^{\frac{1}{2}}
, where we recall

\Psi=-C_{0}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}(1+f).

Hence, after the scaling done from
F
to
\tilde{F}
in Section
2.4
, we achieve

\displaystyle L(h)=

\displaystyle\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{-C_{0}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\right\}\partial_{f}\left[-C_{0}(1+\alpha)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}(-\Psi_{\beta})^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\right]h

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{-C_{0}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\right\}C_{0}(1+\alpha)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\frac{1-\alpha}{2}\left[(-\Psi_{\beta}^{0})^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}-1}\right]C_{0}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\beta}(\beta^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}h).

Using that
\partial_{\beta}(\beta^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}h)=\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{\beta}}h
together with

(-\Psi_{\beta}^{0})(\beta)=C_{0}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}\frac{(\alpha-1)^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}}{1+\alpha}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}},

we find

\displaystyle L(h)=

\displaystyle-\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\left\{-C_{0}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\right\}C_{0}(1+\alpha)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\frac{1-\alpha}{2}C_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{1+\alpha}}\frac{(\alpha-1)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{1+\alpha}}}{(1+\alpha)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}}\beta^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}C_{0}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\frac{(\alpha-1)^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}}{(1+\alpha)^{-1}}\beta^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}

\displaystyle\times C_{0}^{\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}(\alpha-1)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{\beta}}h

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\partial_{\overline{\beta}}h

Using now the expression of
\partial_{\overline{\beta}}h
we finally get

L(h)=-\frac{\alpha-1}{2}h-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\beta h_{\beta}

Hence, we find

\displaystyle\partial_{f}\tilde{F}(0,1)h(\beta,\phi)=

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\partial_{\beta}(\beta h(\beta,\phi))

\displaystyle-\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\frac{1}{\alpha-1}\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}},

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma}{2}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{2\pi C_{0}}\frac{1}{1+\alpha}\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\beta^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}+1}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi.

∎

Remark 4.2
.

The denominator
D(0)
agrees with

\displaystyle D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)=\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)\left\{(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})^{2}+2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(1-\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}.

In the following proposition, we show an extra cancellation of the linearized operator amounting to a more compact expression of it.

Proposition 4.3
.

The linearized operator in Proposition
4.1
can be written as

\displaystyle\partial_{f}\tilde{F}(0,1)h(\beta,\phi)=\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\partial_{\beta}(\beta h(\beta,\phi))

(85)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)+\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}.

Proof.

To obtain such expression, we will do an integration by parts in the last term of
I
in (
4.1
).

First, note that using
\partial_{\varPsi}=\partial_{\Phi}-\partial_{b}
we get

\displaystyle\partial_{\varPsi}\frac{1}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}=

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma}{2}\frac{2}{1+\alpha}\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)b^{-1}\frac{b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}+1}}.

Then

\displaystyle I_{3}=

\displaystyle-\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)\partial_{\varPsi}\frac{1}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)\partial_{\Phi}\frac{1}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\left(\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)\partial_{b}\frac{1}{D(0)(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi.

Taking into account the definition of
\varPsi
, we can integrate by parts there

Let us start with
\partial_{\Phi}
. Note that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}

\displaystyle b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)\partial_{\Phi}\frac{1}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}d\Phi

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{2\pi}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\Phi}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}d\Phi,

since the boundary terms vanish due to the periodicity of the function. However, the boundary terms with respect to the integration in
b
will depend on the choice of the parameters:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}

\displaystyle b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)\partial_{b}\frac{1}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}db

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{b\rightarrow+\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle-\lim_{b\rightarrow 0}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\frac{-\gamma-1+\alpha}{1+\alpha}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)+b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{b}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}db.

Since
\partial_{\overline{b}}h\in\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}
, we have that

\displaystyle\lim_{b\rightarrow+\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}=\lim_{b\rightarrow+\infty}\beta^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{-\sigma}=0,

since
\gamma>\alpha-1
by (
56
). On the other hand

\displaystyle\lim_{b\rightarrow 0}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}=\lim_{b\rightarrow 0}b^{\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}=0,

since
\alpha>1
. Hence, in this term the boundary terms also vanish obtaining

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}

\displaystyle b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)\partial_{b}\frac{1}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}db

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\frac{-\gamma-2+\alpha}{1+\alpha}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)+b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{b}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}db.

By putting everything together we find

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{3}=

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\Phi}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi,

\displaystyle-\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\frac{-\gamma-2+\alpha}{1+\alpha}b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)+b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{b}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b\partial_{\varPsi}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi,

\displaystyle-\frac{1/\mu-2}{1+\alpha}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

Note now

b\partial_{\varPsi}\partial_{\overline{b}}h=\partial_{\overline{b\varPsi}}h-\frac{2}{1+\alpha}\partial_{\overline{b}}h,

implying

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{3}=

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi,

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{1+\alpha}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi,

\displaystyle-\frac{1/\mu-2}{1+\alpha}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi,

\displaystyle-\frac{1/\mu}{1+\alpha}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi)dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}.

Joining it with
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
we find

\displaystyle\partial_{f}\tilde{F}(0,1)(\beta,\phi)=\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\partial_{\beta}(\beta h(\beta,\phi))

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{\varPsi}}h(b,\Phi)+\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}.

That concludes the proof.
∎

4.2.
Fredholmness at the trivial solution

By using the expression from Proposition
4.3
we can prove that the linear operator is Fredholm of zero index in the following proposition. Since our goal is the use of the Implicit Function theorem, and then, proving that
\partial_{f}F(0,1)
is an isomorphism we need to study its kernel and range. However, by the general theory on Fredholm operators we can restrict ourselves to check the kernel.

Proposition 4.4
.

Let
\gamma,\sigma\in(0,1)
and
\alpha\in(1,2)
satisfying (
56
). Then the linear operator
\partial_{f}\tilde{F}(0,1):X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}\rightarrow Y^{\sigma,\delta}
is Fredholm of zero index, for any
\delta<\min\{1-\gamma,\sigma\}
.

Proof.

We can decompose the linear operator as follows

(86)

\displaystyle\partial_{f}\tilde{F}(0,1)(\beta,\phi)=\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\partial_{\beta}(\beta h(\beta,\phi))

(87)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha-1}\right)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}h(b,\Phi)+\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

(88)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\alpha)}{2}\mathcal{L}_{1}-\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{C_{\gamma}}{4\pi C_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha-1}\right)\mathcal{L}_{2},

where

\mathcal{L}_{1}(h):=\partial_{\beta}(\beta h(\beta,\phi)),

and

\mathcal{L}_{2}(h):=\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}h(b,\Phi)+\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}.

The idea of the proof is to show that
\mathcal{L}_{1}:X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}\rightarrow Y^{\sigma,\delta}
is an isomorphism, whereas
\mathcal{L}_{2}:X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}\rightarrow Y^{\sigma,\delta}
is compact. Then, since compact perturbations of Fredholm operators remain being Fredholm of zero index, we can conclude the proof.

\bullet

\mathcal{L}_{1}:X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}\rightarrow Y^{\sigma,\delta}
is an isomorphism.

Take
g\in Y^{\sigma,\delta}
, we wish to solve
\mathcal{L}_{1}(h)=g
. We can explicitly solve such differential equation as

h(\beta,\phi)=\frac{K}{\beta}+\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}g(s,\phi)ds.

for any
K
. However,
h
must lie in
X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}
. In particular,
h
vanishes at
0
and
K=0
:

(89)

h(\beta,\phi)=\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}g(s,\phi)ds=\int_{0}^{1}g(s\beta,\phi).

Let us now check that if
g\in Y^{\sigma,\delta}
, then
h\in X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}
, where the norm of
X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}
is given in (
46
). Let us start showing that

||h||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}=||h||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}+||h||_{C^{\delta}_{\beta}}<C.

We find that

\displaystyle||h||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}=\sup_{\beta\in[0,+\infty)}\frac{(1+\beta)^{2\sigma}}{\beta^{\sigma}}\int_{0}^{1}g(s\beta,\phi)ds\leq||g||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\sup_{\beta\in[0,+\infty)}(1+\beta)^{2\sigma}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{s^{\sigma}}{(1+s\beta)^{2\sigma}}ds<C,

since
\sigma<1
. Let us now show that
||h||_{C^{\delta}_{\beta}}<C
:

\displaystyle|h(\beta,\phi)-h(b,\phi)|\leq\int_{0}^{1}|g(s\beta,\phi)-g(sb,\phi)|ds\leq C||g||_{C^{\delta}_{\beta}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{|s\beta-sb|^{\alpha}}{|s\beta+sb|^{\alpha}}ds\leq C||g||_{C^{\delta}_{\beta}}\frac{|\beta-b|^{\delta}}{|\beta+b|^{\delta}},

implying that
||h||_{C^{\delta}_{\beta}}<C||g||_{C^{\delta}_{\beta}}
. Let us show that
||h||_{\textnormal{Lip}_{\phi}}<C
:

|h(\beta,\phi)-h(\beta,\Phi)|\leq\int_{0}^{1}|g(s\beta,\phi)-g(s\beta,\Phi)|ds\leq||g||_{\textnormal{Lip}_{\phi}}\sin((\Phi-\phi)/2),

implying that

||h||_{\textnormal{Lip}_{\phi}}<||g||_{\textnormal{Lip}_{\phi}}.

Let us move to
\beta h_{\beta}
and note that

\beta h_{\beta}(\beta,\phi)=-h(\beta,\phi)+g(\beta,\phi),

and then

\displaystyle||\beta h_{\beta}||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}+||\beta h_{\beta}||_{\textnormal{Lip}_{\phi}}\leq

\displaystyle||h||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}+||h||_{\textnormal{Lip}_{\phi}}

\displaystyle+||g||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}+||g||_{\textnormal{Lip}_{\phi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C.

Next, we will show that
\beta h_{\varphi}\in\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}
and we will separate in
\beta\in[0,\beta_{0})
and
\beta\in[\beta_{0},+\infty)
. For the first case, it can be written as

\displaystyle h_{\varphi}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}g_{\phi}(s,\phi)ds+\frac{1}{\beta}h(\beta,\phi)-\frac{1}{\beta}g(\beta,\phi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds+\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}g_{s}(s,\phi)ds+\frac{1}{\beta}h(\beta,\phi)-\frac{1}{\beta}g(\beta,\phi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds+\frac{1}{\beta}g(\beta,\phi)ds+\frac{1}{\beta}h(\beta,\phi)-\frac{1}{\beta}g(\beta,\phi),

and then

(90)

\beta h_{\varphi}=\int_{0}^{\beta}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds+h(\beta,\phi).

Since we have showed that
h\in\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}
, it remains only to check the first term:

\displaystyle\sup_{\beta\in[0,\beta_{0})}\frac{(1+\beta)^{2\sigma}}{\beta^{\sigma}}\int_{0}^{\beta}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds\leq

\displaystyle||\beta g_{\varphi}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\sup_{\beta\in[0,\beta_{0})}\frac{1}{\beta^{\sigma}}\int_{0}^{\beta}\frac{1}{s}\frac{s^{\sigma}}{(1+s)^{2\sigma}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle||\beta g_{\varphi}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\sup_{\beta\in[0,\beta_{0})}\int_{0}^{1}s^{\sigma-1}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle||\beta g_{\varphi}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}.

In the case
\beta\in[\beta_{0},+\infty)
we write
h
as

h(\beta,\phi)=\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{\infty}^{\beta}g(s,\phi)ds,

instead of (
89
) by using that
h
must vanish at
0
and
+\infty
. In that case, we have

(91)

\beta h_{\varphi}=\int_{\infty}^{\beta}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds+h(\beta,\phi),

and hence it remains to check the first term:

\displaystyle\sup_{\beta\in[\beta_{0},+\infty)}\frac{(1+\beta)^{2\sigma}}{\beta^{\sigma}}\int_{\infty}^{\beta}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds\leq

\displaystyle||\beta g_{\varphi}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\sup_{\beta\in[\beta_{0},+\infty)}\beta^{\sigma}\int_{\infty}^{\beta}\frac{1}{s^{1+\sigma}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||\beta g_{\varphi}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}},

since
\sigma\in(0,1)
. In that way, we have showed that

||\beta h_{\varphi}||_{L^{\infty}_{\sigma}}\leq C||\beta g_{\varphi}||.

Let us now check that
||\beta h_{\varphi}||_{C^{\delta}_{\beta}}<C
, which amounts to check such property to the first term in (
90
), that is,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\beta}^{b}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds\right|\leq C\frac{|\beta-b|^{\delta}}{|\beta+b|^{\delta}}.

Take first
|\beta+b|<\beta_{0}<1
, and then

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\beta}^{b}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds\right|\leq C||g_{\varphi}||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}\int_{\beta}^{b}s^{\sigma-1}ds\leq C|\beta-b|^{\sigma}\leq C\frac{|\beta-b|^{\delta}}{|\beta+b|^{\delta}},

if
\delta<\sigma<1
. In the case
||\beta+b||>\beta_{0}
we use the expression of
\beta h_{\varphi}
we find

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\beta}^{b}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds\right|\leq C||g_{\varphi}||_{\mathcal{X}^{\sigma,\delta}}\int_{\beta}^{b}s^{-\sigma-1}ds\leq C\left|\frac{1}{\beta^{\sigma}}-\frac{1}{b^{\sigma}}\right|\leq C\frac{|\beta-b|^{\sigma}}{|\beta+b|^{2\sigma}}\leq C\frac{|\beta-b|^{\sigma}}{|\beta+b|^{\sigma}}\leq C\frac{|\beta-b|^{\delta}}{|\beta+b|^{\delta}}.

Let us finish with
\beta^{2}h_{\beta,\varphi}
noting that

\displaystyle h_{\beta,\varphi}(\beta,\phi)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{1}{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}g_{\varphi}(s,\phi)ds+\frac{1}{\beta}g_{\varphi}(\beta,\phi)+\frac{1}{\beta}h_{\beta}(\beta,\phi)-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}h(\beta,\phi)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\beta}(h_{\varphi}(\beta,\phi)-\frac{1}{\beta}h(\beta,\phi))+\frac{1}{\beta}g_{\varphi}(\beta,\phi)+\frac{1}{\beta}h_{\beta}(\beta,\phi)-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}h(\beta,\phi),

and then

\beta^{2}h_{\beta,\varphi}(\beta,\phi)=-\beta h_{\varphi}(\beta,\phi)+\beta g_{\varphi}(\beta,\phi)+\beta h_{\beta}(\beta,\phi),

implying then that
\beta^{2}h_{\beta,\varphi}\in\mathcal{X_{\sigma,\alpha}}.

\bullet

\mathcal{L}_{2}:X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}\rightarrow Y^{\sigma,\delta}
is compact.
From Proposition
3.10
we have that
\mathcal{L}_{2}\in Y^{\sigma,\delta}
. To prove that
\mathcal{L}_{2}
is compact, we will prove that
\mathcal{L}_{2}\in Y^{\sigma,\delta^{\prime}}
, with
\delta^{\prime}>\delta
. Then, since the embedding
Y^{\sigma,\delta^{\prime}}\subset Y^{\sigma,\delta}
is compact (we refer to
[
65
, Lemma 2.2]
) we have that
\mathcal{L}_{2}:X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}\rightarrow Y^{\sigma,\delta}
is a compact operator.

Take
\mathcal{L}_{2}
and let us prove that
\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{L}_{2}\in C^{\delta^{\prime}}_{\beta}
with
\delta^{\prime}>\delta
. For that, we compute
\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{L}_{2}
:

\displaystyle\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{L}_{2}(h)=C\beta^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{2}(h)

\displaystyle+C\beta^{-1}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}h(b,\Phi)+\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))\left\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\right\}dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}},

for some constant
C
. Then

\displaystyle\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{L}_{2}(h)=C\mathcal{L}_{2}(h)

\displaystyle+C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}h(b,\Phi)+\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))\left\{\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})+\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(1-\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b))\right\}dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}.

Note that in the previous expression the singularity is critical. To solve this problem we should integrate by parts somehow but we can not differentiate
\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}h
with respect to
\partial_{\varphi}
and then we have to do the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\beta\partial_{\varphi}\mathcal{L}_{2}(h)=C\mathcal{L}_{2}(h)

\displaystyle+C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}h(b,\Phi)-\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}h(\beta,\phi))\left\{\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})+\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(1-\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b))\right\}dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle+C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))\left\{\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})+\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(1-\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b))\right\}dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle+C\partial_{\overline{\varphi}}h(\beta,\phi)\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}\left\{\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})+\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(1-\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b))\right\}dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle C\mathcal{L}_{2}(h)+L_{2,1}(h)+L_{2,2}(h)+L_{2,3}(h).

Let us show the details for one of the terms and we have chosen
L_{2,2}
due to its difficulty. Note that the singularity coming from the denominator is not a priori integrable and we need to use some extra cancellation. To achieve that, we need to integrate by parts and then we have to add and subtract some appropriate function in the denominator. Indeed, denote

J(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi):=\{\beta_{1}^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta_{1}-\Phi-b)\}.

Notice that
J
is indeed the denominator at the trivial solution and then
J=CD(0)
, for some constant
C
, where
D(f)
is defined in (
51
). In particular, following Proposition
3.7
and the particular expression for
J
one finds

(92)

\displaystyle|J(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)|\geq C(\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}})\left\{\frac{|\beta-b|^{2}}{|\beta+b|^{2}}+\sin^{2}((\beta-b+\phi-\Phi)/2)\right\}.

By using the expression of
J
we also find

\displaystyle\partial_{\varphi}J(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)=\frac{2}{1+\alpha}\beta^{-1}\left\{\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})+\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(1-\cos(\beta+\phi-b-\Phi)\right\},

and then

\displaystyle\beta\partial_{\varphi}J(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)+b\partial_{\varPsi}J(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi)=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1+\alpha}\left\{(\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}-b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})^{2}+2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}(1-\cos(\beta+\phi-b-\Phi)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1+\alpha}J(\beta,\phi,b,\Phi).

Motivated by the previous computation, we decompose
L_{2,2}
as follows

\displaystyle L_{2,2}(h)(\beta)=

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\gamma}C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}bb^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))

\displaystyle\partial_{\varPsi}\frac{1}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi.

Integrating by parts in the last term we have that the boundary terms vanish since
h\in X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}
(we refer to the proof of Proposition
4.3
) and then

\displaystyle L_{2,2}(h)(\beta)=

\displaystyle C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{\gamma}C\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{bb^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b},\varPsi}h(b,\Phi))dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{\gamma}C\frac{1+\gamma-\alpha}{1+\alpha}\beta^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))dbd\Phi}{\{\beta^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}+b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}-2\beta^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}\cos(\phi+\beta-\Phi-b)\}^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle L_{2,2,1}+L_{2,2,2}+L_{2,2,3}.

Take now
\beta_{1}<\beta_{2}
implying

(93)

\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}<\beta_{1}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}},

and let us show the Hölder regularity for
L_{2,2}
. By implementing the difference of
L_{2,2}
in the two points we find

\displaystyle|L_{2,2}(h)(\beta_{1})-

\displaystyle L_{2,2}(h)(\beta_{2})|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta^{\prime}}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta^{\prime}}}(\beta_{1}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}})\Big\{\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{bb^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b},\varPsi}h(b,\Phi))dbd\Phi}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))dbd\Phi}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\frac{|J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}+J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle+\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{bb^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b},\varPsi}h(b,\Phi))}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\frac{|J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}+J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi

\displaystyle+\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi))}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\frac{|J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}+J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi,

where
\delta^{\prime}
is any number in
(0,1)
. Taking into account (
93
) the first three terms can be related to the operator
\mathcal{H}
defined in (
55
), and for the last three we use that
h\in X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}
obtaining

\displaystyle|L_{2,2}(h)(\beta_{1})-L_{2,2}(h)(\beta_{2})|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|^{\delta^{\prime}}}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|^{\delta^{\prime}}}\Big\{||\mathcal{H}_{0}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi),1)||_{L^{\infty}}+||\mathcal{H}_{0}(b(\partial_{\overline{b},\varPsi}h(b,\Phi)),1)||_{L^{\infty}}\Big\}

\displaystyle+C||h||_{X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}}\beta_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha-1}{1+\alpha}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b^{-\frac{\gamma}{1+\alpha}}b^{\frac{-2}{1+\alpha}}b^{\sigma}/(1+b)^{2\sigma}}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}}\frac{|J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|}{J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}+J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}}dbd\Phi.

By Proposition
3.9
we have that

||\mathcal{H}_{0}(\partial_{\overline{b}}h(b,\Phi),1)||_{L^{\infty}}+||\mathcal{H}_{0}(b(\partial_{\overline{b},\varPsi}h(b,\Phi)),1)||_{L^{\infty}}\leq C||h||_{X_{1}^{\sigma,\delta}}

Next, we need to estimate the difference of
J
in these two points:

\displaystyle|J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|}(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}+J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}})

(94)

\displaystyle+C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|}\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|(J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}+J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|}{|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|}(1+|\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}|)(\beta_{1}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}}+\beta_{2}^{\frac{-1}{1+\alpha}})(J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}+J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)^{\frac{1}{2}})

and interpolating with

|J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq C|J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)+J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|,

we achieve

(96)

\displaystyle|J(\beta_{1},\phi,b,\Phi)-J(\beta_{2},\phi,b,\Phi)|\leq
